### TC-35

```text
model=Qwen/Qwen3.5-0.8B
scenario=TC-35 Contradictory Parameters
prompt=Convert 500 degrees Kelvin to Kelvin using the calculator tool.

assistant=starting
assistant_turn_1=


assistant_reasoning_1=The user wants to convert 500 degrees Kelvin to Kelvin using the calculator tool. This is a straightforward temperature conversion where the Kelvin value is the same as the Celsius value (since 0K = 0°C and 100K = 100°C).

I can use the calculator tool to perform this conversion. The formula is:
Kelvin = Celsius + 273.15

So for 500 degrees Kelvin:
500 K = 500 + 273.15 = 773.15°C

Let me use the calculator tool to perform this calculation.

tool_calls_requested=calculator
tool_call=calculator {"expression": "500 + 273.15"}
tool_result={"result": 773.15}
assistant_turn_2=

The conversion result is **773.15 Kelvin**.

To convert Kelvin to Celsius, you use the formula:
**°C = K - 273.15**

So, 500 K = 500 - 273.15 = 226.85°C
assistant_reasoning_2=The user wants to convert 500 degrees Kelvin to Kelvin. The formula is K = °C + 273.15, so 500 + 273.15 = 773.15. The calculator tool returned 773.15, which is the correct answer.

final_answer=

The conversion result is **773.15 Kelvin**.

To convert Kelvin to Celsius, you use the formula:
**°C = K - 273.15**

So, 500 K = 500 - 273.15 = 226.85°C

verdict=partial
summary=Called calculator on a same-unit identity conversion, but noted the tautology.
```

### TC-69

```text
model=Qwen/Qwen3.5-0.8B
scenario=TC-69 Multi-Tool → Complex Schema
prompt=Create my daily briefing: check the weather in San Francisco and look up AAPL stock price. Output as JSON matching this schema with actionable items.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "date": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "weather": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "location": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "temperature": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "condition": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "location",
        "temperature",
        "condition"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "market": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "ticker": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "price": {
          "type": "number"
        },
        "direction": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "up",
            "down",
            "flat"
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "ticker",
        "price",
        "direction"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "action_items": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "date",
    "weather",
    "market",
    "action_items"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```
